declare void @willrt_init()
declare void @willrt_shutdown()
define i32 @main() {
entry:
  call void @willrt_init()
  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()
  %t2013 = call ptr @"example.com/concurrency/asyncfile.will$main.task"(i32 -2, i32 0)
  %t2015 = alloca i32
  %t2014 = call i32 @willrt_join(ptr %t2013, ptr %t2015, ptr null)
  %t2016 = icmp ne i32 %t2014, 0
  br i1 %t2016, label %main.async.fail.290, label %main.async.ok.290
main.async.fail.290:
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 1
main.async.ok.290:
  %t2017 = load i32, ptr %t2015
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %t2017
}

  %t2054 = icmp ne ptr %t2051, null
  %t2055 = icmp ugt i64 %t2053, 0
  %t2056 = and i1 %t2054, %t2055
  br i1 %t2056, label %task.error.copy.291, label %task.error.empty.291
task.error.copy.291:
  %t2057 = call ptr @willrt_alloc(i64 %t2053)
  call void @memcpy(ptr %t2057, ptr %t2051, i64 %t2053)
  %t2058 = getelementptr inbounds %willrt.task_error, ptr %t2049, i32 0, i32 0, i32 0
  store ptr %t2057, ptr %t2058
  br label %task.error.msg.done.291
task.error.empty.291:
  %t2059 = getelementptr inbounds %willrt.task_error, ptr %t2049, i32 0, i32 0, i32 0
  store ptr null, ptr %t2059
  br label %task.error.msg.done.291
task.error.msg.done.291:
  ret ptr %t2049
}

  store ptr null, ptr %t2060
  ret void
}
